## Schedule 3 — Pay and conditions of Academy teachers: amendments to the [Education Act 2002](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/contents)

###### 1

[Part 8](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/part/8) of the [Education Act 2002](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/contents) (teachers’ pay and conditions etc) is amended as follows.

###### 2

In [section 120](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/120)[(2)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/120) (School Teachers’ Review Body function: meaning of school teacher), for the words from “the Secretary of State’s” to the end substitute “section 122 or an Academy teacher for the purposes of [section 122A](#p16516).”

###### 3

In [section 121](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/121)[(2)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/121) (bodies to be consulted by School Teachers’ Review Body), after [paragraph (b)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/121) insert—

> > (ba) bodies representing the interests of proprietors of Academies,

###### 4

In the heading of [section 122](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/122), after “conditions” insert “of school teachers other than Academy teachers”.

###### 5

After [section 122](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/122) insert—

> > (122A)
>   (1) The Secretary of State may by order make provision requiring the remuneration of an Academy teacher to be at least equal to the amount specified in, or determined in accordance with, the order.
>   (2) [Subsection (3)](#p16535) applies where—
>     (a) an order under this section applies to an Academy teacher, and
>     (b) the contract of employment or for services between the Academy teacher and the relevant proprietor provides for the teacher to be paid remuneration that is less than the amount specified in, or determined in accordance with, the order.
>   (3) Where this subsection applies—
>     (a) the Academy teacher’s remuneration is to be determined and paid in accordance with any provision of the order that applies to the teacher;
>     (b) any provision of the contract mentioned in [subsection (2)](#p16522)[(b)](#p16530) or of the Academy arrangements entered into with the Secretary of State by the relevant proprietor has no effect to the extent that it makes provision that is prohibited by, or is otherwise inconsistent with, the order.
>   (4) A person is an Academy teacher for the purposes of this section in any of the following cases.
>   (5) The first case is where—
>     (a) the person provides primary or secondary education under a contract of employment or for services,
>     (b) the other party to the contract is the proprietor of an Academy,
>     (c) the contract requires the person to carry out work of a kind which is specified by regulations under section 133(1), and
>     (d) the person—
>       (i) is not prevented by regulations under section 133(1) from carrying out that work, and
>       (ii) is not of a description specified in regulations made by the Secretary of State for the purposes of this paragraph.
>   (6) The second case is where the person—
>     (a) serves as the principal of an Academy, and
>     (b) is not appointed by the proprietor of the Academy as an executive leader of the proprietor.
>   (7) The third case is where the person would fall within section 122(5) but for the fact that the other party to the contract of employment or for services under which the person provides primary or secondary education is the proprietor of an Academy (and not a party mentioned in section 122(3)(c)).
>   (8) Regulations under [subsection (5)](#p16554)[(d)](#p16569) may, in particular, specify a description by reference to a person’s duties or to any provision for a person’s remuneration to be determined otherwise than under this section.
>   (9) Where the proprietor of an Academy is also the proprietor of a 16 to 19 Academy, a person (“*P*”) is not an Academy teacher for the purposes of this section to the extent that a contract of employment or for services between P and the proprietor requires P to provide secondary education at the 16 to 19 Academy.
>   (10) In the application of [subsections (2)](#p16522) and [(3)](#p16535)—
>     (a) it is immaterial whether someone other than the relevant proprietor provides or is responsible for providing all or part of a teacher’s remuneration;
>     (b) it is immaterial whether someone other than the relevant proprietor is treated wholly or partly as a teacher’s employer for some or all purposes by virtue of an enactment.
>   (11) In this section “*the relevant proprietor*”, in relation to an Academy teacher, means the proprietor mentioned in [subsection (5)](#p16554)[(b)](#p16561), [(6)](#p16582)[(b)](#p16589) or [(7)](#p16594) (as the case may be).

###### 6

In [section 122A](#p16516) (inserted by [paragraph 5](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2026/21/schedule/3/paragraph/5)), after [subsection (10)](#p16609) insert—

> > (10A) In determining the conditions of employment or service of an Academy teacher, the relevant proprietor must have regard to any provision of an order under section 122 that relates to conditions of employment or service (and must also have regard to guidance under section 127(1) that relates to such conditions).

###### 7

In [section 123](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/123) (scope of section 122 orders)—

- (a) in the heading, after “122” insert “or [122A](#p16516)”;

- (b) after [subsection (1)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/123) insert—

> > (1A) Subsection (1) applies in relation to an order under [section 122A](#p16516) as it does in relation to an order under section 122 but as if—
>   (a) the reference in paragraph (a) to a local authority or a governing body were to a proprietor of an Academy, and
>   (b) paragraphs (f) to (h) were omitted.

- (c) in [subsection (2)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/123)[(b)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/123), after “local authorities” insert “, teachers and proprietors of Academies”;

- (d) in [subsection (3)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/123), after “122” insert “or [122A](#p16516)”;

- (e) in [subsection (4)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/123), after [paragraph (c)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/123) insert—

> > (d) that a payment or entitlement of a specified kind is or is not to be treated as remuneration for the purpose of [section 122A](#p16516)[(1)](#p16518).

###### 8

###### 9

In [section 125](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/125)[(1)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2002/32/section/125) (requirement to refer matter before making order), after “122” insert “or [122A](#p16516)”.

> > (ba) bodies representing the interests of proprietors of Academies,

###### 11

>   (1) In sections 121 to 127, a reference to an Academy—

> > (3A) Where, by virtue of sub-paragraph (2), paragraph 21(1) ceases to apply to any proposals approved by the Secretary of State under paragraph 8, those proposals must be considered afresh by the Secretary of State under that paragraph (and paragraph [17](#p17155) applies accordingly).
